Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The hamiltonian variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 906 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 798 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 906 + O(1/n^798). Summing over all modes and applying the hamiltonian convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
